## Prerequisites to resolve (231)

Each entry: the bead that needs something, the need as its drafter wrote it, and candidate carriers. Where the
need names its carrier by key, the keys named are listed: confirm each and decide dep, related or none. Otherwise
the candidates were found mechanically (by plan section, gate and title words): suggestions, not answers.

### BD-078 (M1) frob-sense: the clock contract: rational lattice, phase accumulators, the exact schedule (sense.schedule)
- **need 1**: §42 OPEN-SENS-2: the owner's ruling on the lattice limit and the menu of declared rationals for common device rates (default in force: Q at most 2⁴⁰, and the proposed menu of this bead)
  - `N20b-24` (M2, U20b, task) decision OPEN-SENS-2: the clock-lattice limit and the menu of declared rationals for common device rates
  - `N25d-48` (M1, U25d, task) frob-conformance: the Augur open-question register (OPEN-AUGUR-1 to -11) and the defaults-in-force battery
  - `N41-05` (M0, U41, task) Owner decision OPEN-PROG-1: repository visibility, the licence file, and who outside the program can replicate the result
  - `N41-14` (M0, U41, task) Recorded owner decision OPEN-SUITE-2 and OPEN-TETHER-1: one toolchain T for the whole build graph, frob-mcp in the core graph; confirmation at M0
  - `N21c-35` (M0, U21c, task) Owner decision OPEN-SPARK-2: the compute envelope E0 to E3; until then one device and one hour per GPU job
  - `N31b-26` (M5, U31b, task) Decision OPEN-FOUNDRY-3: the current-capacity table for conductor sizing; default density against the card limit
  - `N39b-31` (M1, U39b, task) Owner decision OPEN-TENFOLD-6: the common agent of the ten-fold test: model, revision, token and tool-call caps
  - `N21c-38` (M2, U21c, task) Owner decision OPEN-SPARK-5: no Metal binding of our own by default; branches if the gateway fails admission
  - `N39b-27` (M2, U39b, task) Owner decision OPEN-TENFOLD-2: who the adjudicator of the ten-fold test is, or the self-adjudicated default
  - `N23b-25` (M2, U23b, task) Decision OPEN-CORTEX-2: the default action law; with OPEN-CORTEX-5, the default discount rates recorded
